# Photos that still need the photographer's permission

SnakeList shows ads, so every photo on it must allow commercial use: CC0, public domain, CC BY or CC BY-SA (always with credit). Photos under a "non-commercial" licence (CC BY-NC, CC BY-NC-SA) or with no licence can only stay if the photographer says yes.

In November 2026 every SnakeList species photo was checked. Where a good, correctly identified, openly licensed photo of the same species existed (iNaturalist research-grade sightings, Wikimedia Commons), it replaced the old one. The photos below had no such replacement, so they are still on SnakeList with their old licence. For each one, ask the photographer (message below). If they say yes, write `"permission": "granted <date>"` for that species in `data/photo-permissions.json` and run `python3 tools/photo_licences.py`. If they say no, the photo has to come off (the species then shows "no photo yet").

**1096 photos by 828 photographers** still need permission (plus the first opening photo, see the end).

Tip: one message per photographer, listing all of their photos, is quicker for them. On iNaturalist, open the photographer's profile (`https://www.inaturalist.org/people/<username>`) and use **Message**.

## Message to send

Subject: Permission to use your snake photo on SnakeList

> Hi {name},
>
> I help my son run SnakeList (https://snakelist.com), a free website about all the world's snake species, made for snake fans and kids. We love your photo of the {species}: {link}
>
> We would like to show it on that species' page on SnakeList, with your name as the photographer and a link back to your observation. The site carries some ads to pay for its costs, and your photo's licence (CC BY-NC) does not allow that without your OK. Would you be happy for SnakeList to use it, with credit? (If you prefer, changing the photo's licence on iNaturalist to CC BY would also work.)
>
> If you would rather we did not use it, that is completely fine. Just say so and we will take it down.
>
> Thank you very much!
> {your name}, for SnakeList

For a photographer with several photos, replace the second paragraph's photo with the list of their photos from the table.

## The first opening photo

The Smooth Green Snake opening photo (`species-photos/hero/rough-green-hq-*.webp`, shown on a first visit) was supplied without a photographer's name or licence. Find out where it came from and ask that photographer too, or replace it with one of the other (credited, openly licensed) opening photos. It also has a Christmas photo-hat entry (`tools/christmas/photo-hats.json`), which would need redoing for a replacement.
